	private void SyncUnityHooks()
	{
		SyncEventListeners();
		if (!(null == owner_Connection_1) && m_RegisteredForEvents)
		{
			return;
		}
		owner_Connection_1 = parentGameObject;
		if (null != owner_Connection_1)
		{
			uScript_CustomUScriptEvent uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2 = owner_Connection_1.GetComponent<uScript_CustomUScriptEvent>();
			if (null == uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2)
			{
				uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2 = owner_Connection_1.AddComponent<uScript_CustomUScriptEvent>();
			}
			if (null != uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2)
			{
				uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2.uScriptStart += Instance_uScriptStart_0;
			}
		}
	}

	private void RegisterForUnityHooks()
	{
		SyncEventListeners();
		if (!m_RegisteredForEvents && null != owner_Connection_1)
		{
			uScript_CustomUScriptEvent uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2 = owner_Connection_1.GetComponent<uScript_CustomUScriptEvent>();
			if (null == uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2)
			{
				uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2 = owner_Connection_1.AddComponent<uScript_CustomUScriptEvent>();
			}
			if (null != uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2)
			{
				uScript_CustomUScriptEvent2.uScriptStart += Instance_uScriptStart_0;
			}
		}
	}

	private void SyncEventListeners()
	{
	}

	private void UnregisterEventListeners()
	{
		if (null != owner_Connection_1)
		{
			uScript_CustomUScriptEvent component = owner_Connection_1.GetComponent<uScript_CustomUScriptEvent>();
			if (null != component)
			{
				component.uScriptStart -= Instance_uScriptStart_0;
			}
		}
	}

	public void Start()
	{
		SyncUnityHooks();
		m_RegisteredForEvents = true;
	}

	public void OnEnable()
	{
		RegisterForUnityHooks();
		m_RegisteredForEvents = true;
	}

	public void OnDisable()
	{
		UnregisterEventListeners();
		m_RegisteredForEvents = false;
	}

	private void UpdateEditorValues()
	{
		uScript_MasterComponent.LatestMasterComponent.UpdateNodeValue("phone_guide.uscript:player", local_player_CharacterGfx);
		uScript_MasterComponent.LatestMasterComponent.UpdateNodeValue("71f680d7-edf3-4ae2-a742-f69fd6683e25", local_player_CharacterGfx);
	}

	private bool CheckDebugBreak(string guid, string name, ContinueExecution method)
	{
		if (m_Breakpoint)
		{
			return true;
		}
		if (uScript_MasterComponent.FindBreakpoint(guid))
		{
			if (!(uScript_MasterComponent.LatestMasterComponent.CurrentBreakpoint == guid))
			{
				uScript_MasterComponent.LatestMasterComponent.CurrentBreakpoint = guid;
				UpdateEditorValues();
				Debug.Log("uScript BREAK Node:" + name + " ((Time: " + Time.time + string.Empty);
				Debug.Break();
				m_ContinueExecution = method.Invoke;
				m_Breakpoint = true;
				return true;
			}
			uScript_MasterComponent.LatestMasterComponent.CurrentBreakpoint = string.Empty;
		}
		return false;
	}
}
